# T042 — Reconcile every real API and RPC boundary row

[Feature group](../group.md) · [Overall plan](../../../06-PLAN.md)

## Purpose and scope

Matrix: all28 storage families/260 column rows plus36 GraphQL and45 RPC rows have their complete physical, owner, projection, retention, allowed/denied/presence/deadline/error/side-effect results. ResolveActor remains deny-only on selected network callers. This task owns the change surface and local evidence below. Risk is high because permission, retained state, irreversible effects or a real service boundary can fail without an obvious UI error. Review the task-specific negative and recovery cases before coding. Unrelated features and source-document changes are outside this task.

## Prerequisites

- [T007 — Connect account and session browser journeys](../../03-account-access/tasks/T007-connect-account-and-session-browser-journeys.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Access: Visitor/Viewer/Maintainer see the permitted navigation and direct protected routes enforce current access; Viewer cannot register or choose a management role.
- [T008 — Enforce scoped service identity and shared admission](../../06-guarded-access/tasks/T008-enforce-scoped-service-identity-and-shared-admission.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Identity: forged/wrong-audience/stale-role/revoked assertions fail before business work; valid mTLS alone never grants actor authority.
- [T013 — Connect discovery, details and public people views](../../07-catalog-discovery/tasks/T013-connect-discovery-details-and-public-people-views.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Search: a delayed old reply cannot replace current criteria; invalid 201-code-point input stays correctable without a truncated request; browser Back restores complete criteria.
- [T016 — Prove recovery through real movie, person and credit editors](../../08-draft-recovery/tasks/T016-prove-recovery-through-real-movie-person-and-credit-editors.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Real recovery: after committed save loses response, resume reveals the original outcome and current record without creating another movie/person/credit.
- [T018 — Connect movie editors and confirmed outcomes](../../09-movie-management/tasks/T018-connect-movie-editors-and-confirmed-outcomes.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Editor: valid create/update displays committed values under the same ID; optional omissions stay unknown and retained invalid fields remain fully correctable.
- [T020 — Connect people management and retained edits](../../10-people-and-credits/tasks/T020-connect-people-management-and-retained-edits.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Management: permitted A creates/edits a shared person and sees credit projections update; Viewer direct management remains denied.
- [T022 — Connect credit editing and verify the people outcome](../../10-people-and-credits/tasks/T022-connect-credit-editing-and-verify-the-people-outcome.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Journey: A creates person and role, assigns two different roles, edits characters then removes one credit; public detail shows exact surviving relationship without cloning.
- [T024 — Connect private archives and irreversible confirmations](../../12-archive-management/tasks/T024-connect-private-archives-and-irreversible-confirmations.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Privacy: Visitor/Viewer cannot read archives through UI, old URL, direct API or related credits; Maintainer can inspect retained content.
- [T027 — Connect measured progress and recover attachment outcomes](../../11-artwork-management/tasks/T027-connect-measured-progress-and-recover-attachment-outcomes.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Progress: observed byte events drive transfer only; processing remains visible until actual attachment and current movie image agree.
- [T028 — Expose complete read-only audit outcomes](../../14-accountable-history/tasks/T028-expose-complete-read-only-audit-outcomes.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Attribution: history contains actor/action/affected record/time/outcome/relevant changes for each included event; no absent fields are inferred from scalar compatibility.
- [T033 — Verify ChatGPT approval and complete both-client acceptance](../../13-assisted-movie-creation/tasks/T033-verify-chatgpt-approval-and-complete-both-client-acceptance.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, ChatGPT: actual human action is bound to the exact displayed content and cannot be forged through tool parameters or retrieved instructions.
- [T035 — Initialize and replay seed and prepared accounts safely](../../15-reproducible-seed/tasks/T035-initialize-and-replay-seed-and-prepared-accounts-safely.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Fresh: two clean isolated installations have equivalent accepted identities/metadata and exact initial100/100 with usable prepared roles.
- [T036 — Record actionable alerts through one selected mode](../../16-operational-notifications/tasks/T036-record-actionable-alerts-through-one-selected-mode.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Default: exhausted eligible work yields one inspectable sanitized recording and zero sends; database mode needs no external provider credential.
- [T039 — Implement the seven reproducible lifecycle commands](../../18-local-operations/tasks/T039-implement-the-seven-reproducible-lifecycle-commands.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, One command: documented prerequisites plus make start yield usable local seeded app and operational entry points without manual migrate/seed/start steps.
- [T040 — Rotate keys and aliases without losing access](../../19-protected-recovery/tasks/T040-rotate-keys-and-aliases-without-losing-access.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Continuity: existing and new account/draft access works through rotation with correct values and no plaintext leakage.
- [T041 — Verify migrations, purge and coordinated restore](../../19-protected-recovery/tasks/T041-verify-migrations-purge-and-coordinated-restore.md): consume its accepted implementation, declared contracts/change surface and local evidence. Specifically, Migration: drift/partial incompatible schema blocks readiness with diagnostic cause, and rollback does not pretend to undo destructive committed change.

All executable commands below are proposed interfaces produced by T001; none exists at planning time. Use its isolated fixture/runtime and protected synthetic access. Normal unfinished dependencies are execution prerequisites, not evidence of a failed implementation. Keep any newly discovered material contract incompatibility visible with affected source IDs and next action.

## Change surface

All listed implementation paths are proposed. Existing source documents remain read-only. Paths resolve from repository `app`.

| Repository | Action | Path | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|---|
| app | create | tests/integration/src/test/kotlin/com/spoolin/integration/contracts/ | T042 owns the task-specific code/configuration/tests within this target; preserve the declared feature boundary |
| app | create | tests/contracts/release-matrix/ | T042 owns the task-specific code/configuration/tests within this target; preserve the declared feature boundary |

Tests follow the owner paths above and the source test map. Frontend unit/component tests are colocated within the declared feature/component paths; JVM units use the declared src/test paths. The cross-service tests/integration project belongs only to the integration selector. If a source path has moved, record the actual producer/consumer mapping before continuing; do not silently broaden the task.

| [BRAIN-D21 · L90](../../../01-brainstorming-result.md#4-decision-register) | D21 \| Committed \| Bounded exponential backoff with jitter for safe transient failures; environment-configurable retry policy and exhaustion notification through one configured channel |
| [BRAIN-D25 · L94](../../../01-brainstorming-result.md#4-decision-register) | D25 \| Committed \| Categorize movies and TV series by title type and genres, include synopses and useful descriptive metadata, and retain stable identities and source provenance to support future review aggregation; detailed baseline in section 8.2 |

## Implementation

1. Reconcile all28 storage case families ATDD-223–250 and their260 physical-column rows, including the complete Verification continuation paragraphs: owner-valid crypto/permission/version rules, actual projections, host binding, initialization and retention/purge are separate required subproofs. Collect earlier narrow producer results, then execute or join missing real-boundary results at this task; physical SQL acceptance alone cannot close the parent case.
2. Implement/collect a result for each ATDD-251 GraphQL operation row and ATDD-252–255 RPC method row against packaged real owners. Keep rows and allowed/denied SAN/actor/presence/side-effect subvariants independently addressable.
3. Feature tasks own their row-specific handlers/narrow proof; this task reconciles the parent matrices and adds missing cross-boundary composition, not a duplicate implementation or generic test replacing the source oracles.
4. Verify wire→DTO→JPA/native projection and generated compatibility, omission/null/precision, deadlines/cancellation, query/message/envelope caps and safe error mapping. Read operations add no mutation-only requirement or success audit.
5. Observe prohibited effects through fresh owner reads and exact result fields; mTLS, compilation and unit mocks are insufficient for authoritative permission/transaction assertions.
6. Close G-02 only with source-current real PostgreSQL18.6/TCP gRPC/filesystem evidence and reconciled storage-column reports, including historical-name rejection and current contract identity.

## Development guidance

Follow [shared execution rules](../../../06-PLAN.md#shared-execution-rules). Keep feature command/query/validation/projection/persistence close, constructor dependencies explicit and DTOs immutable. Generated contracts are not entities or authorization. Do not import another service's repository, copy a schema, invent a fourth domain service, or bypass the owner with fixture SQL.

Use parameter binding, fixed identifiers/allowlisted projections and safe text sinks at the relevant boundary. Keep account secrets, raw files, CSRF, approval challenges and private payloads out of retained evidence. New source policy requires a recorded owner decision; an unavailable client/runtime is not permission to weaken acceptance. Do not alter expected values, delete negative rows or replace visual references merely to make checks pass.

## Acceptance

- **T042-A1:** Matrix: all28 storage families/260 column rows plus36 GraphQL and45 RPC rows have their complete physical, owner, projection, retention, allowed/denied/presence/deadline/error/side-effect results. ResolveActor remains deny-only on selected network callers.
- **T042-A2:** Compatibility: exact declared values and safe failures survive real boundaries without private entity leakage or loss of scalar precision.
- **T042-A3:** Evidence: a missing required row/runtime produces incomplete/setup status, never Passed; source hashes and executing revision match.

Verification ownership is scoped: the exact cases and case rows in Tests require their full stated boundary. Shared requirement/AC/contract clauses require this task’s owned contribution and local assertions; their combined outcome is reconciled by the case, feature and release owners. An implementation-only contribution stops at its owned change and local tests; a downstream full-case result is not a condition for this task to finish.

## Tests

Develop the integration/host verification alongside assembly of its real fixtures and evidence. The feature producers already own rule tests; this task does not require an artificial failing baseline when their behavior is correct. Add a failing regression before correcting any newly discovered defect, then rerun the affected proof. Derive expected values independently from source fixtures, not implementation output.

Use [ATDD fixtures and approach](../../../05-atdd.md#approach-and-fixtures) and [C-RESET](../../../05-atdd.md#c-reset-shared-cleanup). Each case/table/recipe subrow resets its own F-STACK/F-ACTORS state; pin time/randomness and use observable barriers for races rather than sleep-only synchronization. Preserve sanitized failure and fresh-reader state, then clean only that UUID namespace.

Partial/wrong phase groups and mismatched outcome mapping reject23514; duplicate same attempt phase or recovery generation rejects23505. SQL permits references but cannot establish matching STARTED or current fence: owner locks work, validates original tuple and rejects stale completion before appending. Claim/STARTED and completion/FINISHED/work/local result each roll back together on failure. Watchdog retains original tuple/UNCERTAIN evidence and reconciles destination before any new send. Matching phase history survives unresolved/exhausted state and purges together only when eligible. Runtime UPDATE/DELETE is denied; no public/model/event-row projection exists. Retention: 90 days for resolved history; never purge events while their work remains exhausted, uncertain or awaiting controlled disposition. Lifetime Catalog business events use the separate table. | [ATDD-249](../../../05-atdd.md#case-atdd-249-enforce-multiple-protected-artwork-reference-promises) / Integration | F-DB; Several RESERVED/ACTIVE/RELEASED references to one existing asset and separate title/intent tuples. Supply n+1 characters as an untruncated bound insert value to test physical length separately, without an explicit narrowing cast that can truncate. This proves declared storage rules, with separate owner/API checks for policy, permissions, cryptographic validity and cross-row invariants. It does not grant application authority from direct SQL. \| Column / SQL type \| Independent physical probe \| Exact declared storage contract \| \| --- \| --- \| --- \| \| `id` (`uuid`) \| F-DB lowercase, hyphenated UUID; malformed UUID fails conversion, not a CHECK; Omission fails23502; explicit NULL fails23502 \| `PRIMARY KEY; no SQL default; owner supplies value` \| \| `asset_id` (`uuid`) \| F-DB lowercase, hyphenated UUID; malformed UUID fails conversion, not a CHECK; Omission fails23502; explicit NULL fails23502 \| `NOT NULL REFERENCES asset(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT; no SQL default; owner supplies value` \| \| `title_id` (`uuid`) \| F-DB lowercase, hyphenated UUID; malformed UUID fails conversion, not a CHECK; Omission fails23502; explicit NULL fails23502 \| `NOT NULL; no SQL default; owner supplies value` \| \| `state` (`varchar(16)`) \| Source-valid technical token; 16 versus 17 PostgreSQL characters tests physical capacity independently of any declared allowlist; no configured user-text rule inferred; Omission fails23502; explicit NULL fails23502 \| `NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('RESERVED','ACTIVE','RELEASED')); no SQL default; owner supplies value` \| \| `catalog_version` (`bigint`) \| Source-valid integer; physical upper 9223372036854775807 versus 9223372036854775808 tests range separately from row acceptance; declared CHECK probes -1, 0, 1; Omission yields SQL NULL subject to table/variant predicates; explicit NULL is physically nullable, subject to table/variant predicates (rejection is23514) \| `CHECK (catalog_version >= 0); NULL allowed; no SQL default; owner supplies value` \| \| `created_at` (`timestamptz(6)`) \| F-CLOCK UTC instant with six fractional digits; roundtrip microseconds. Finite-wire/expiry policy is owner validation unless DDL declares it; Omission supplies CURRENT_TIMESTAMP; explicit NULL fails23502 \| `NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP; NOT NULL (explicit or PK)` \| \| `updated_at` (`timestamptz(6)`) \| F-CLOCK UTC instant with six fractional digits; roundtrip microseconds. Finite-wire/expiry policy is owner validation unless DDL declares it; Omission supplies CURRENT_TIMESTAMP; explicit NULL fails23502 \| `NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP; NOT NULL (explicit or PK)` \| Probe asset FK23503, hold ID23505, state allowlist and ACTIVE catalog_version requirement23514. Multiple distinct references per asset are accepted. Catalog title/intent IDs are logical and need authorized reconciliation. Under real owner locks, RESERVED unknown stays protected without time-only expiry; RELEASED is terminal and stale version/messages cannot resurrect it. Cleanup never unlinks bytes with any active/unresolved protective reference or removes another title’s attachment. | [ATDD-252](../../../05-atdd.md#case-atdd-252-verify-identityservice-typed-service-and-actor-boundary) / Contract | F-RPC-ALLOWLIST full method × verified SAN matrix, plus each required human/connector/tuple authorization condition; use the typed request/response matrix below for payload rows. | Then typed permitted outcomes follow the operation contract And invalid channel/actor/method/presence/range inputs return mapped safe statuses and no prohibited effect And transport cancellation/deadline is observed at the real receiver Observation: Execute every F-RPC-ALLOWLIST row for IdentityService over actual TCP mTLS with separate SAN certificates. Capture receiver status, denied private projections and absent prohibited effects before business dispatch. Allowed service pairs still require the row’s live actor/connector/tuple predicates; generated type compatibility alone does not prove access. The payload matrix below is exercised independently under its allowed caller, or deny-only for ResolveActor. \| Method \| Typed request → response \| \| --- \| --- \| \| `ResolveActor` \| ResolveActor(ResolveActorRequest) → Actor \| \| `CheckActor` \| CheckActor(CheckActorRequest) → Actor \| Known routes: Core calls Catalog public/managed/writes; Catalog calls Core CheckActor before protected commit; Core calls Media upload/read; Catalog workers call Media reserve/confirm/release/receipt; Media calls only Catalog GetAttachment; MCP calls only Core gateway. The MCP SAN cannot directly invoke Catalog/Media service-only methods or Identity directory access. F-RPC-ALLOWLIST now supplies the complete selected method/SAN decisions; no broader route is inferred. |

Unit/component tests prove only isolated logic or the controlled component. PostgreSQL constraints/locks/commit/crash tests use PostgreSQL18.6 and fresh independent reads; transport tests use actual TCP mTLS/gRPC; browser/client and provider claims need their named real boundary. Direct DDL probes prove only declared SQL constraints, not permission, policy or cryptographic validity.

## Review and evidence

The implementer checks the diff and runs affected tests. One independent reviewer then inspects the complete requested selection, including this task, relevant source contracts, coherent connections, actual results, security and accessibility. Reference the same selection review from each task's independent-review row; no separate task or intermediate-group review is required. Record current inputs, distinct implementer/reviewer identities, required findings and fixes in one ledger. Correct in the same branch and rerun affected checks; self-review cannot replace final independent review.

Visual review is not applicable: this owned result has no new graphical presentation. Any consumer UI/host proof remains with its explicitly assigned task; service/CLI evidence observes responses, state, files and diagnostics.

## Completion and handoff

- Finish the owned changes and all verification-role acceptance/row assertions; record actual changed files and source-contract dispositions.
- Reach Ready instructions and Passed evidence for every required check, including implementation diff review, the shared final selection review and applicable behavior/visual checks. Any unresolved required oracle prevents the affected task from Done.
- Record exact revision, generated/artifact locations, commands/results, findings/rechecks, fixture cleanup and any unverified downstream proof.
- Hand the owned outputs to [T046 — Prepare the reviewed repository and release handoff](T046-prepare-the-reviewed-repository-and-release-handoff.md) for feature integration. This task may finish its local obligations while that downstream evidence remains Not run; feature completion waits for all group tasks plus integrated proof.
- If unfinished, retain status In progress/In review or a genuine external Blocked prerequisite; list the exact unresolved finding, last verified state, attempted fixes and next file/command/action. Budget exhaustion alone is not an external blocker.
- Initial next action: read the prerequisite outputs, verify source fingerprints and task eligibility, then perform Implementation step1 under the separately authorized implementation request.
